416	def interpolator2D(_x, _y, _z, grid=False):     x = _x.copy()     y = _x.copy()     z = _z.copy()     if grid:         (x, y) = np.meshgrid(x, y)         x = x.reshape(x.size)         y = y.reshape(y.size)         z = z.reshape(z.size)     points = np.vstack([x, y]).transpose()     itp = CloughTocher2DInterpolator(points, z)     return itp

3367	def polySci(x, cf,order):     """ Polynomial correction of the science layer.     Build the polynomial corrected_img = a0 + a1 * x + a2 * x^2 + a3 * x^3 + a4 * x^4 """      #to be explicit, make a deep copy of input x and var because only the un-masked pixles are modified         corrected_img = np.zeros_like(x)         for i in range(0,order):         corrected_img += cf[i] * np.power(x, i)     return corrected_img
